{"parentUuid":"390a000b-918b-4aaf-8139-ae829438a5e6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"50f7732a-a167-4585-934c-509ff83ada17","agentId":"a7057b619c35e5ee1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AGooZZGm49dhmE9FLqGkst","type":"tool_result","content":"                // Ensure socket client is disconnected\r\n                if (_isConnectedToSocketServer)\r\n                {\r\n                    await _socketClientService.DisconnectFromServer();\r\n                    _isConnectedToSocketServer = false;\r\n                }\r\n                \r\n                // Verify device connection status after error\r\n                if (_serialService.IsConnected())\r\n                {\r\n                    _currentDeviceInfo.IsConnected = true;\r\n                    _loggerService.LogMessage($\"Device status: {Constants.DeviceInfo.STATUS_CONNECTED} despite proxy error\");\r\n                }\r\n                else\r\n                {\r\n                    _connectedPort = string.Empty;\r\n                    _currentDeviceInfo.IsConnected = false;\r\n                    _loggerService.LogMessage($\"Device status: {Constants.DeviceInfo.STATUS_DISCONNECTED} after proxy error\", isError: true);\r\n                }\r\n                \r\n                return false;\r\n            }\r\n        }\r\n        \r\n        /// <summary>\r\n        /// Synchronizes data with the NovaLum device using sync socket server as a proxy\r\n        /// </summary>\r\n        /// <returns>True if sync completed successfully</returns>\r\n        public async Task<bool> SynchronizeData()\r\n        {\r\n            try\r\n            {\r\n                // First, make sure any existing restarting state is cleared\r\n                // if it's been lingering from a previous sync\r\n                if (IsDeviceRestarting() && (DateTime.Now - _restartTimestamp).TotalSeconds > 5)\r\n                {\r\n                    _loggerService.LogMessage(\"Resetting lingering device restart state before sync\");\r\n                    SetDeviceRestarting(false);\r\n                }\r\n\r\n                // Prepare for sync by ensuring connection is in a clean state\r\n                _loggerService.LogMessage(\"Preparing for sync operation by refreshing device connection...\");\r\n                \r\n                // Get the current port before potentially disconnecting\r\n                string currentPort = _connectedPort;\r\n                \r\n                // Refresh serial connection to ensure clean state for sync\r\n                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(currentPort))\r\n                {\r\n                    _loggerService.LogMessage($\"Refreshing connection on port {currentPort}\");\r\n                    _serialService.DisconnectPort();\r\n                    \r\n                    // Short delay to allow port to reset\r\n                    await Task.Delay(500);\r\n                    \r\n                    // Reconnect on same port\r\n                    if (!_serialService.ConnectToPort(currentPort))\r\n                    {\r\n                        _loggerService.LogMessage(\"Failed to reconnect before sync - attempting device detection\", isError: true);\r\n                        if (!await DetectDevice())\r\n                        {\r\n                            _loggerService.LogMessage(\"Device detection failed - cannot continue with sync\", isError: true);\r\n                            return false;\r\n                        }\r\n                    }\r\n                    else\r\n                    {\r\n                        _loggerService.LogMessage($\"Successfully refreshed connection on port {currentPort}\");\r\n                        _connectedPort = currentPort;\r\n                        \r\n                        // Verify we have a NovaLum device\r\n                        if (!_serialService.VerifyNovaLumDevice())\r\n                        {\r\n                            _loggerService.LogMessage(\"Failed to verify NovaLum device after reconnection\", isError: true);\r\n                            return false;\r\n                        }\r\n                    }\r\n                }\r\n                else\r\n                {\r\n                    // No current connection, attempt full detection\r\n                    _loggerService.LogMessage(\"No existing connection found, performing full device detection\");\r\n                    if (!await DetectDevice())\r\n                    {\r\n                        _loggerService.LogMessage(\"Device detection failed - cannot continue with sync\", isError: true);\r\n                        return false;\r\n                    }\r\n                }\r\n                \r\n                // Now delegate to the more comprehensive StartProxyConnection method\r\n                return await StartProxyConnection(GetServerInfo());\r\n            }\r\n            catch (Exception ex)\r\n            {\r\n                _loggerService.LogMessage($\"Error preparing for sync: {ex.Message}\", isError: true);\r\n                return false;\r\n            }\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        private int GetTypeSize(string commandType)\r\n        {\r\n            if (Constants.DataTypes.Sizes.TryGetValue(commandType, out int size))\r\n                return size;\r\n            return 16; // Default size\r\n        }\r\n        \r\n        /// <summary>\r\n        /// Sets the device restarting status and sets a timestamp\r\n        /// This method is used by both internal code and the SocketClientService to indicate when the device is restarting\r\n        /// </summary>\r\n        /// <param name=\"isRestarting\">Whether the device is currently restarting</param>\r\n        public void SetDeviceRestarting(bool isRestarting)\r\n        {\r\n            lock (_restartLock)\r\n            {\r\n                _deviceRestarting = isRestarting;\r\n                if (isRestarting)\r\n                {\r\n                    _restartTimestamp = DateTime.Now;\r\n                    _loggerService.LogMessage(\"Device restart mode activated - connection error messages will be suppressed\");\r\n                }\r\n                else\r\n                {\r\n                    _loggerService.LogMessage(\"Device restart mode deactivated - normal connection status checking resumed\");\r\n                }\r\n            }\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        /// <summary>\r\n        /// Checks if the device is currently in restarting state\r\n        /// If the restart happened more than 30 seconds ago, automatically reset the state\r\n        /// </summary>\r\n        /// <returns>True if device is restarting, false otherwise</returns>\r\n        public bool IsDeviceRestarting()\r\n        {\r\n            lock (_restartLock)\r\n            {\r\n                // If restarting mode has been active for more than the timeout period, automatically reset it\r\n                if (_deviceRestarting && (DateTime.Now - _restartTimestamp).TotalSeconds > Constants.DeviceInfo.RESTART_TIMEOUT_SECONDS)\r\n                {\r\n                    _deviceRestarting = false;\r\n                    _loggerService.LogMessage(\"Device restart mode timeout - normal connection status checking resumed\");\r\n                }\r\n                \r\n                return _deviceRestarting;\r\n            }\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        /// <summary>\r\n        /// Tests the socket connection to the sync server using the address and port from global info\r\n        /// </summary>\r\n        /// <returns>True if connection test was successful</returns>\r\n        public async Task<bool> TestSocketConnection()\r\n        {\r\n            try\r\n            {\r\n                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_connectedPort))\r\n                {\r\n                    _loggerService.LogMessage(\"No device connected\", isError: true);\r\n                    return false;\r\n                }\r\n                \r\n                // Check if we have valid server info\r\n                if (_currentDeviceInfo == null || \r\n                    string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentDeviceInfo.SyncServerAddress) || \r\n                    _currentDeviceInfo.SyncServerPort <= 0)\r\n                {\r\n                    _loggerService.LogMessage(\"Missing server information. NovaLum device expects plain data\n                _serialPort.Write(data, 0, data.Length);\n                _loggerService.LogMessage($\"Data sent to device: UNENCRYPTED as required by NovaLum device\");\n\n                // Allow some time for the data to be written\n                await Task.Delay(50);\n\n                return true;\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                _loggerService.LogMessage($\"Error writing data: {ex.Message}\", isError: true);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Sends a command directly to the device without standard error checking\n        /// This method is specifically for the restart command when we already know the device is about to disconnect\n        /// </summary>\n        /// <param name=\"data\">Command data to send</param>\n        public void SendCommandWithoutErrorChecking(byte[] data)\n        {\n            // This method bypasses the normal error checking for the restart command\n            // We don't care if the serial port is connected or not, since the device may already be restarting\n            try\n            {\n                if (_serialPort != null && _serialPort.IsOpen)\n                {\n                    // FIXED: NovaLum device requires unencrypted data, so we send data as-is\n                    // The restart command must be sent unencrypted to the device\n                    \n                    // Send data directly without encryption - NovaLum device expects plain data\n                    _serialPort.Write(data, 0, data.Length);\n                }\n                // Deliberately no else branch - we don't want to log errors during restart\n            }\n            catch\n            {\n                // Deliberately swallow exceptions - the device might already be disconnecting\n                // The caller handles any logging needed\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"24e99b07-1d15-47d8-9d81-877474379eee","timestamp":"2026-03-22T17:08:33.396Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"92b9077c-8641-4788-a6c2-e99dd9e139e8","userType":"external","cwd":"C:\\Users\\acer","sessionId":"0a446eb4-6983-4540-8d7a-a52ea990ac67","version":"2.1.76","gitBranch":"master","slug":"tender-forging-naur"}
